HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000745/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2a8c89fdc17998be3d8b","text":"3. **A named authority that blocks autonomous elevation.** `س م و:B005` makes `اسم` a raised designation, and `ر ب ب:B001` supplies ownership and obeyed authority. This invocation precedes the repeated reading command, whereas 96:6 presents the human crossing bounds. I infer that acting `باسم ربك` assigns the act’s authority and elevation to the Rabb rather than to the reader. The phrase becomes an anti-autonomous condition on knowledge: recitation is elevated by whose designation it bears, not by the speaker’s self-importance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3552b0ad3a47f820be9b","text":"4. **A created measure against which transgression becomes legible.** `خ ل ق:B001` provides proportion, while `خ ل ق:B004` provides formed disposition and moral bearing. The window moves from creation and teaching to the human’s `يطغى` in 96:6, whose `ط غ ي:B001` is overstepping a bound. The supplied facts are measure and later boundary-crossing; I infer that the created proportion supplies the normative line being crossed. `خلق` consequently includes the establishment of an apt order that human conduct can violate, without implying that creation forces the violation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_001210/B001","root_001211/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_38121ca6ce0a5c1b59de","text":"96:4–5 made the gathering variant of `ق ر ء:B001` newly concrete by supplying pen-made signs and acquired knowledge to assemble. The sharper surprise came in 96:6: `يطغى` retrospectively turned the measuring branch `خ ل ق:B001` into a moral boundary and `باسم ربك` into protection against self-authorized elevation. This corrected an initially narrower reading of `خلق` as origin alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_000434/B002","root_000434/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_44346958fbfd3d2a4e57","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:1:5\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yaratma, yalnızca var etme değil, ölçü koyma ve insana biçim kazandırmadır; sonraki sınır aşımı bu ölçünün ihlali olarak okunur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_78c0217c07682be5754f","text":"96:19 retrospectively changed the first imperative: the opening “read” and the closing “prostrate and draw near” mirror each other as authorized acts against a competing command. The later “do not obey him” makes `باسم ربك` sound like allegiance, not only formula.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_000532/B002","root_001210/B001","root_001211/B001","root_001290/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8dba819d88a78d26439e","text":"1. “Read” becomes an act of gathering speech under a raised name. Evidence: `ق ر ء B001` gathering speech into recitation, `ق ر ء B002` reading/teaching recitation, `س م و B005` name as raised designation, `ر ب ب B001` lordship/authority. Structural cue: `ٱقْرَأْ` is immediately attached by `بـ` to `ٱسْم`, and `رَبِّكَ` stands between name and creation. Mechanism: gathered utterance is not self-originating; it is organized by the name and authority of the Lord. Abductive move: the packet gives the prepositional binding and branch inventory; I infer that “with/by the name” supplies the operative authorization of the reading. Reading change: the imperative reads less like “pronounce words” and more like “assemble utterance under a named authority.” 2. “Who created” activates measured formation as well as origination. Evidence: `خ ل ق B001` measuring/proportioning, `خ ل ق B002` bringing into being, `خ ل ق B004` inner disposition, with later `خ ل ق B007` fabricated speech activated by 96:13/16 `ك ذ ب B001/B002`. Structural cue: the creator is named before the created human appears in 96:2 and before denial appears in 96:13. Mechanism: true creation includes proportion and inner disposition; false speech later imitates creation by fabricating its own account. Abductive move: the packet supplies “created” and later “lied”; I infer a contrast between divine proportioning and human fabrication. Reading change: the ayah reads as a summons to recite from the source of real formation, against later pseudo-formation by denial. 3. The Lord is also the nurturer who brings stages to completion. Evidence: `ر ب ب B002` repair/nurture/completion and `ر ب ب B003` knowledge formed by care. Structural cue: 96:1 `رَبِّكَ` is resumed in 96:3 and 96:8, while 96:4-5 explains teaching. Mechanism: creation, reading, and teaching form a care-chain. Abductive move: the packet gives repeated Lord + creation + teaching; I infer that “your Lord” is not bare sovereignty but stage-by-stage cultivation. Reading change: the command becomes pedagogical and formative, not only authoritative.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B002","root_000434/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9e6252242f56dcfa2ea5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:1:5\",\"root_000434\",[\"B002\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gerçek biçim vermenin Allah'a ait oluşu, ilerideki yalanlamayı sözle sahte bir gerçeklik kurma girişimi olarak karşısına alır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000532/B002","root_000532/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b471436556258ece29e0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:1:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rab, buyuran bir sahip olmanın yanında, yaratmayı öğretimle sürdürüp aşama aşama olgunlaştıran yetiştiricidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001210/B001","root_001211/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ceda0b2191df89698676","text":"1. **Recitation as the gathering of a whole.** The gathering-speech variant of `ق ر ء:B001` joins collecting with articulated recitation. In 96:2–5, material origin, repeated `اقرأ`, pen, and previously unknown knowledge are placed around the command. The branch’s role is to make reading an assembly operation: dispersed signs, origin, and taught content are gathered into ordered utterance. The packet gives the sequence; I infer that the repeated imperative asks for integrative articulation rather than bare vocalization. `اقرأ` therefore reads as “gather into a recitable whole” as well as “recite.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_eca4530bc1ab00bb984e","text":"2. **Creation as measured, continuing nurture.** `خ ل ق:B001` supplies measuring and proportioning before execution, `خ ل ق:B002` originating, and `ر ب ب:B002` repair, nurture, and stage-by-stage completion. The relative clause binds `ربك` directly to `خلق`, while 96:2 specifies a dependent material beginning and 96:4–5 continue the process through instruction. I infer that the creator’s initial proportioning continues as the Rabb’s developmental care. The clause thus reads less like a past credential and more like the opening phase of sustained formation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
